### R128. General shared-orientation exact rank

Use actual original block x=c+A u+B v+G d, all binary, [A B G] full column rank, a=dim u, b=dim v, delta=dim d. This holds for direct complements of the shared translation space in the original R108 regions. Up to separable row/column signs, original weighted pairing is
    M_z(u,v)=sum_(d in F2^delta) (-1)^(h dot d) prod_i z_i^(c_i xor A_i u xor B_i v xor G_i d).
Define the two row matroids L=[B G] and R=[A G] on the SAME original coordinate set E. Let nu_* be their maximum common independent set size, equivalently
    nu_* = min_(I disjoint J=E) [rank([B G]_I)+rank([A G]_J)].
Then for ANY coefficient field K, over K(z_1,...,z_w),
    rank M_z = 2^(nu_*-delta).
This includes arbitrary shared sign h and characteristic two. Full column rank guarantees nu_*>=delta; nu_*<=delta+min(a,b). R122 is delta=0. R124 upper 2^(delta+nu) is refined since rank([B G]_I)<=rank(B_I)+delta and likewise A. The exponent is invariant under separately changing region bases and adding shared-coordinate combinations to chosen complements. Indeed [B G] and [A G] represent actual full right/left symmetry spaces, independent of complement choice. Row-coordinate weight labels cannot be freely changed by arbitrary linear rechart.

### R129. Upper bound: actual joint affine source across a cut, no independent shared charge

For any partition I,J, consider the affine relation of pairs
    l=(u,x_I), r=(v,x_J), x=c+A u+B v+G d.
The map (u,v,d)->(l,r) is injective because G full column rank. Its affine dimension is a+b+delta. Left projection dimension is a+rank([B G]_I); right projection dimension b+rank([A G]_J). An arbitrary affine relation between two vector spaces decomposes into disjoint complete bipartite blocks between cosets of its left-only/right-only kernels: for linear relation T<=L0 direct_sum R0, define K_L={l:(l,0) in T}, K_R={r:(0,r) in T}; matching quotient classes P_L(T)/K_L and P_R(T)/K_R are isomorphic by unique partner class. Affine anchor supplies the translated identification. Number of blocks =2^kappa, kappa=dim P_L+dim P_R-dim T. Thus here
    kappa(I)=rank([B G]_I)+rank([A G]_J)-delta.
Extend h on shared d to a binary character ell on original x with ell A=ell B=0 and ell G=h, possible by full column rank. Sign (-1)^(h dot d)=(-1)^(ell dot c) (-1)^(ell_I dot x_I) (-1)^(ell_J dot x_J). Each complete bipartite block contributes a separated product after summing the left/right actual projections with original local weights and signs. There is NO multiplicity error: original (u,v,d) corresponds bijectively to admissible pairs (l,r). This proves a factorization of M through 2^kappa channels over ANY commutative ring, no Fourier divisions, and exact weighted scalar
    Z=(-1)^(ell dot c) sum_k L_k R_k,
    L_k=sum_(u,x_I in left block k) f(u)(-1)^(ell_I dot x_I)prod_(i in I) z_i^x_i,
    R_k=sum_(v,x_J in right block k) g(v)(-1)^(ell_J dot x_J)prod_(i in J) z_i^x_i.
Gaussian elimination gives actual projection and kernel bases and shared quotient labels without expanding entire relation. Given explicit messages, enumerate each projection independently, accumulate keyed sums, cost O(poly(w)*(2^(a+rank([B G]_I))+2^(b+rank([A G]_J))+2^kappa)) ring operations plus message access, bit cost and other quotient blocks. Choose minimizing partition or another cost-effective valid partition; minimizing channels need not minimize total work. May be more costly than other scalar contractions. Does not require enumerating every d for every pair u,v. Matroid intersection gives min kappa=nu_*-delta. This is the exact generalization of R122's upper bound.

### R130. Lower bound: common independent certificate pins actual shared coordinates

Take maximum common independent row set C, |C|=nu_*. Then G_C has full column rank delta: otherwise some row G_i outside its span exists by full G rank; adding i to C preserves independence in BOTH augmented matrices, contradiction to maximality. Put Q=F2^C/im G_C of dimension k=nu_*-delta. Because [A G]_C and [B G]_C have full row rank |C|, the induced maps Abar_C, Bbar_C onto Q are BOTH surjective. Specialize z_i=0 on C and z_i=1 elsewhere (formal polynomial specialization, 0^0=1; not a nonzero unramified character sample). Then weighted summand survives exactly if
    c_C+A_C u+B_C v+G_C d=0.
For a given u,v there is exactly one such d iff their quotient syndromes match after the affine offset. Its coefficient is +/-1; the sign separates in u,v by extending h to a form on F2^C. Choose one u,v per quotient syndrome. The resulting 2^k square minor is a signed permutation matrix, determinant unit in EVERY coefficient field. Thus generic polynomial rank >=2^k. Combined with R129 proves R128 exactly. No need to bound cancellations for arbitrary weight samples. A stronger nonzero finite-order realization follows R131. This closes generic D!=0 rank, not a lower bound on global message production or scalar evaluation.

### R131. Common-independent specialization = signed group-algebra convolution, exact odd-prime Smith and binary unit criterion

Keep z_i=1 outside C, arbitrary weights on C; let ell_C be any extension of shared character h, satisfying G_C^T ell_C=h. After separable row/column signs and translation by c_C, duplicate rows/columns reduce by integral unimodular operations to square convolution matrix N on Q. Define
    F(s)=sum_(x_C projects to s) (-1)^(ell_C dot x_C) prod_(i in C) z_i^x_i,
    N_(s,t)=F(s+t).
In group algebra O[Q],
    F = prod_(i in C) (1+(-1)^ell_i z_i [e_i mod im G_C]).
This preserves original shared residual as the actual pushforward of weighted named coordinates, NOT independent invented weights. All quotient characters l in Q^* correspond to linear forms l on F2^C annihilating im G_C. Eigenvalues over characteristic not 2:
    lambda_l = prod_(i in C) (1+(-1)^(ell_i+l_i) z_i).
Det N=prod_l lambda_l as integer polynomial identity (prove over Q(z), then specializes in every commutative ring). Over odd-prime DVR, Walsh matrix is unimodular (2 unit), so EXACT nonzero Smith valuations are sorted {v_p(lambda_l)} (infinity means zero invariant, omitted from nonzero Smith list), including extra zero rows/columns in original matrix. Mod p^H required linear channels of this specialization are #l with valuation <H. This is not an arbitrary-unspecialized Smith theorem. R123 transverse case delta=0 is recovered (for each i valuation of product pairs combines to v_p(1-z_i^2) after reordering and unit conditions; at odd prime with both factors units no depth).

In binary coefficient ring, Walsh inversion need not be integral; use determinant/group algebra instead. Reduction mod 2 in residue field gives det N = (prod_(i in C)(1+z_i))^(2^k), since all signs equal. Therefore N is invertible over the unramified binary DVR or its finite quotients iff every selected z_i residue differs from 1. This is realizable with genuine nonzero finite-order Teichmuller lifts in F_(2^m) for m>=2, outside-C weights 1. Thus R128 full rank is not merely formal specialization at zero; actual unramified weights can realize unit channels. Over odd prime choose residue not +/-1 on selected coordinates in a suitable unramified extension for sufficient unit condition. This does NOT replace R85 randomized support sampling or give a universal hitting set: forcing these coordinates or others to 1 may hide other original witnesses. The coefficient field prime and residue extension remain independent of actual original W_Q3 ramification and Frobenius. At z=1 all original shared orientations inhabited; with trivial h unweighted matrix entries 2^delta, with nontrivial h signed sum cancels. Modulo binary precision<=delta may report zero even in trivial h case. Keep actual source zero mode, signed character residual and sample/precision separately.

### R132. Indexed hyperfabric, G79–83 and actual cost boundary

The new certificate belongs to the actual source block and weight consumer, retaining history, event time tau, refinement/chart k, Frobenius iteration j, version, creation/requested precision, coefficient ring, sample and original named coordinate/affine anchor data. Rank partition, common independent set, shared subspace basis and quotient syndrome labels are dependent residual data. Invertible region basis change preserves both augmented matroids. Compact reproduction of ACTUAL augmentation and rank checks:
```python
from itertools import product
from random import Random
from collections import deque
from math import prod
from sympy import Matrix, ZZ
from sympy.matrices.normalforms import smith_normal_form
def rank(xs):
 b={}
 for x in xs:
  while x:
   p=x.bit_length()-1
   if p in b:x^=b[p]
   else:b[p]=x;break
 return len(b)
def mi(A,B):
 E=set(range(len(A)));S=set()
 def ind(M,T):return rank(M[i] for i in T)==len(T)
 while True:
  O=E-S
  Q=deque(i for i in sorted(O) if ind(A,S|{i}));prev={i:None for i in Q};end=None
  while Q:
   i=Q.popleft()
   if i in O:
    if ind(B,S|{i}):end=i;break
    N=[j for j in S if ind(B,S-{j}|{i})]
   else:N=[j for j in O if ind(A,S-{i}|{j})]
   for j in sorted(N):
    if j not in prev:prev[j]=i;Q.append(j)
  if end is None:
   J=set(prev);I=E-J
   assert rank(A[i] for i in I)+rank(B[i] for i in J)==len(S)
   return S,I,J
  path=set()
  while end is not None:path.add(end);end=prev[end]
  S^=path
  assert ind(A,S) and ind(B,S)
def bit(x):return x.bit_count()&1
def mat(A,B,G,c,h,z,a,b,g):
 return [[sum((-1)**bit(h&d)*prod(z[i]**(bit(A[i]&u)^bit(B[i]&v)^bit(G[i]&d)^(c>>i&1)) for i in range(len(z))) for d in range(1<<g)) for v in range(1<<b)] for u in range(1<<a)]
rng=Random(369)
for a,b,g in product(range(1,4),range(1,4),range(3)):
 for _ in range(12):
  n=rng.randrange(a+b+g,a+b+g+4)
  while True:
   A=[rng.randrange(1<<a) for i in range(n)];B=[rng.randrange(1<<b) for i in range(n)];G=[rng.randrange(1<<g) for i in range(n)]
   if rank([x|(y<<a)|(z<<(a+b)) for x,y,z in zip(A,B,G)])==a+b+g:break
  L=[y|(z<<b) for y,z in zip(B,G)];R=[x|(z<<a) for x,z in zip(A,G)]
  C,I,J=mi(L,R);k=len(C)-g
  assert rank(G[i] for i in C)==g and 0<=k<=min(a,b)
  assert len(C)==max(m.bit_count() for m in range(1<<n) if rank(L[i] for i in range(n) if m>>i&1)==rank(R[i] for i in range(n) if m>>i&1)==m.bit_count())
  c=rng.randrange(1<<n);h=rng.randrange(1<<g)
  N=mat(A,B,G,c,h,[0 if i in C else 1 for i in range(n)],a,b,g)
  assert Matrix(N).rank()==2**k
  z=[rng.randrange(-2,4) for i in range(n)]
  assert Matrix(mat(A,B,G,c,h,z,a,b,g)).rank()<=2**k
```

### R135. Actual ordered interaction algebra and nilpotent residual filtration

Let R be a commutative coefficient ring and let A be the unital R-algebra generated by ALL actual d-by-d transfer matrices (both orientations of each named guard, plus constant transfers). J is the two-sided ideal generated by the commutators of those generators, equivalently the span of A[A,A]A: the quotient by generator commutators is commutative because generators commute there. Actual word length m, original guard count q, each unary weight charged once even for repeated reads. For an actual adjacent swap AB=BA+[A,B], the signed residual belongs to J. A product with r explicit residual factors and arbitrary A factors between lies in J^r. If J^s=0, every term with >=s residuals vanishes exactly. No inference that the source words are equal; the equality is a signed sum of their actual ordered products. Over a field, a nilpotent ideal J of an algebra in M_d has index <=d: chain J^i K^d is descending, and equality before zero implies persistent equality under multiplying by J, incompatible with nilpotence. This proof does not assume algebraically closed field or simultaneous diagonalization.

### R136. Finite signed sorting expansion and shared tensor transfer

Choose any fixed total order of original guard labels, including constant positions at the beginning (constants preserve their relative order). Stable sorting only exchanges different labels, preserving same-label occurrence order. At each adjacent inversion, use the exact two-branch identity AB=BA+[A,B]. On the main branch swap A,B; on the residual branch replace them by the ordered defect [A,B], separating the remaining ordinary word into two segments. Continue stable sorting independently within all segments, NEVER moving factors across a retained defect. Each residual uses its actual original labels/positions and matrix orientations. Drop a branch only when its explicit defect count reaches s (by J^s=0). At depth r, deterministic main sorting has at most B=m(m-1)/2 swaps across all segments; each new residual removes two ordinary occurrences. Hence at most sum_(r=0..s-1) max(1,B)^r terminal terms, each of form
    P_0(x) C_1(x) P_1(x) ... C_r(x) P_r(x),
where each P_t is grouped by increasing original guard label and each C_t is an actual commutator of two source occurrences. This includes constant swaps; no erroneous independent fresh variable for a repeated occurrence.

For a terminal term condition only the original guard labels that appear in retained commutators (<=2r, so <=2^(2r) assignments). This makes C_t fixed matrices. For each segment t and guard i let P_(t,i)(b) be its ordered product of the occurrences with that label (identity if absent). Let constant prefix P_(t,*) be the original-order constant product in segment. The exact tensor of simultaneous segment products, weighted over remaining original labels, is
    K = (tensor_t P_(t,*)) product_(i in label order)
          [sum_(b allowed by pins) w_i(b) tensor_t P_(t,i)(b)].
The tensor matrix dimension is d^(r+1). This formula holds without division, with arbitrary signed or zero weights, over any commutative ring. It uses the fact that the order of labels within ALL segments agrees; multiplying tensor factors coordinatewise gives the actual products, and then summing each original bit once shares all its repeated occurrences. Unused guard weights multiply as w_i(0)+w_i(1). Apply the linear bridge multiplication map on pure tensors, extended linearly:
    Phi_C(X_0 tensor ... tensor X_r)=X_0 C_1 X_1 ... C_r X_r.
Then sum Phi_C(K) over the pinned guard assignments and all terminal terms. No assumption that the segment functions are independent after marginalizing shared guards; the tensor representation is precisely what retains their shared dependence.

Using ordinary dense matrix arithmetic, one upper bound is
    O(sum_(r=0..s-1) max(1,B)^r 4^r poly(m,q,d) d^(3(r+1))).
Polynomial for fixed d,s, and for fixed field matrix dimension d with nilpotent J since s<=d. Signed exact integer bit sizes remain polynomial in explicit word length and coefficient bit sizes at fixed d,s; finite-ring bit costs charged separately. This is an actual algorithmic upper bound, not a required representation or time lower bound. It can be worse than G85 noncrossing, G86 cover, R129 matroid cuts or other scalar circuits; choose cheapest known applicable method. No assumption of efficient global word minimization. A constant-width transfer with arbitrary commutator algebra is not covered.

### R137. Polynomial source certificate and residue-field lifting

Over a field, compute A by linear closure starting from identity and generators, multiplying basis matrices by generators until rank stabilizes. At most d^2 independent matrices. Compute J by starting with actual generator commutators and closing under left/right multiplication by A basis. Compute descending powers J^i via products of basis elements. If J^s=0, valid cutoff; if a nonzero power stabilizes, nilpotence fails. Gaussian elimination and closure use polynomially many field operations in d and explicit generator count; actual source matrices used. No need to find an optimal algebra representation or common eigenbasis.

For coefficient ring O/(p^H), including an unramified extension, a residue-field certificate of nilpotence index s yields a valid bound sH at the higher precision: reduction maps J^s to zero, so J^s consists of matrices divisible by p, and J^(sH) is zero modulo p^H. This is not the assertion that the same s works at every precision. Can exploit R85 direct binary weighted P with H=1 or signed D with H=3 and log-degree unramified coefficient extension; both constant H. For variable H the exponent can grow, not a polynomial runtime guarantee. Coefficient prime remains independent of original ramified W_Q3 and source Frobenius. The finite tests here use scalar Z/p^H, not actual extension-field arithmetic. Canonical lifting residual R96 remains independently recorded; no use of residue vanishing as an exact source zero.

### R138. Explicit fully interleaved genuinely noncommuting cheap source

Take upper unitriangular 3-by-3 matrices
    U(a,b,c)=I+a E12+b E23+c E13.
Actual multiplication U(a,b,c)U(a',b',c')=U(a+a',b+b',c+c'+a b'). Commutators of two such matrices are (a b'-a' b) E13, and all such ideal products of length two vanish. Thus J^2=0 for their generated algebra, even when the guard word is (1,...,q)^3 and every pair interleaves. Concrete nonzero residual [I+E12,I+E23]=E13. For any ordered list, complete product is exactly
    I + (sum_j a_j)E12 + (sum_j b_j)E23
      + (sum_j c_j + sum_(j<k) a_j b_k)E13.
Contract each term by grouping its one or two actual guard labels. For each affected label, sum its two weighted values of the product of all its factors (when labels coincide, same bit!), and multiply unaffected baseline factors w_i(0)+w_i(1). This gives O(m^2 q) ring operations by direct baseline products, division-free even when some baseline factors vanish. Can precompute products excluding any one/two labels by interval products for further speedup; not implemented/benchmarked. This is a stronger negative control than G87 commuting involutions: maximal interleaving, true signed commutators, yet polynomial exact weighted contraction. It is a matrix-transfer example, not necessarily a Boolean deterministic SAT encoding. Preserve source origin and rejected/acceptance semantics only when actual transfer is deterministic.

### R139. Consumer-specific contextual annihilator, not whole-matrix rank

Actual Langlands producer ultimately needs only scalar endpoint lambda(X)=a^T X b. Define
    I_lambda={Z in A : lambda(X Z Y)=0 for all X,Y in A}.
This is a two-sided ideal: left or right multiplying Z merely changes X or Y; closed under sums and scalars. It is the largest two-sided ideal contained in ker(lambda), since any ideal in the kernel absorbs all contexts. Compute it over a field by linear equations against all pairs of A basis matrices. Equivalently, for each power J^s test lambda(X Z Y)=0 on basis triples. If J^s subset I_lambda, the same sorting expansion is exact AFTER applying lambda even if omitted full matrices are nonzero. Every omitted term is contextually unobservable for that specific consumer, not erased source geometry. The quotient A/I_lambda has nilpotent commutator ideal iff such s exists; if nonzero powers stabilize without inclusion, condition fails. Polynomial field certificate via Gaussian basis and powers; at fixed d, s<=dim A+1 sufficient search (nilpotent chain in quotient). We use full tensor bridges in the test for clarity, not an optimized smaller quotient representation. Example block-diagonal transfer with observable triangular block and unobservable full M2 block has nonnilpotent whole J but scalar-specific cutoff <=2. ### R140. Actual G84 acceptance core is nonnilpotent; baseline is a different observer

Pair T-BT=(E12,0), then multiply by B to get (E11,0), left multiply by B to get (E21,0), multiply E21 E12 to get (E22,0); subtract diagonal sum from identity to get (0,1). Reproduction core (ordinary exact modular matrix functions add/sub/mul/scale, identity, zero, Kronecker product; use stable increasing-label order with constants label -1). The actual local implementation:
```python
from itertools import product
from collections import defaultdict
from math import prod
# F=(label,M0,M1), constants label=-1 and M0=M1.
def get(F,x): return F[1+(x.get(F[0],0) if F[0]>=0 else 0)]
def terminal(segs,defects,w,d,p):
 r=len(defects); labels=sorted({F[0] for D in defects for F in D if F[0]>=0})
 out=zero(d)
 for bits in product((0,1),repeat=len(labels)):
  pinned=dict(zip(labels,bits))
  br=[sub(mul(get(A,pinned),get(B,pinned),p),mul(get(B,pinned),get(A,pinned),p),p) for A,B in defects]
  if any(not any(x for row in C for x in row) for C in br):continue
  pre=[];groups=[]
  for seg in segs:
   c=eye(d);g=defaultdict(list)
   for F in seg:
    if F[0]<0:c=mul(c,F[1],p)
    else:g[F[0]].append(F)
   pre.append(c);groups.append(g)
  K=kprod(pre)
  for i in range(len(w)):
   H=zero(d**(r+1))
   for b in ([pinned[i]] if i in pinned else (0,1)):
    M=[]
    for g in groups:
     c=eye(d)
     for F in g.get(i,[]):c=mul(c,F[b+1],p)
     M.append(c)
    H=add(H,scale(kprod(M),w[i][b],p),p)
   K=mul(K,H,p)
  digits=list(product(range(d),repeat=r+1))
  T=[[0]*d for _ in range(d)]
  for I,ii in enumerate(digits):
   for J,jj in enumerate(digits):
    T[ii[0]][jj[-1]]+=K[I][J]*prod(br[t][jj[t]][ii[t+1]] for t in range(r))
  out=add(out,tuple(tuple(x%p for x in row) for row in T),p)
 return out
def contract(word,w,d,p,s):
 def visit(segs,defs):
  r=len(defs);out=zero(d);segs=[list(x) for x in segs]
  for k,seg in enumerate(segs):
   while True:
    i=next((i for i in range(len(seg)-1) if seg[i][0]>seg[i+1][0]),None)
    if i is None:break
    A,B=seg[i:i+2]
    if r+1<s:
     new=segs[:k]+[seg[:i],seg[i+2:]]+segs[k+1:]
     out=add(out,visit(new,defs[:k]+[(A,B)]+defs[k:]),p)
    seg[i:i+2]=[B,A]
  return add(out,terminal(segs,defs,w,d,p),p)
 return visit([word],[])
```

The induced literal matrices are I,Y=[[0,1],[0,1]], and boundary R=[[0,0],[1,0]]. For literal truth values L_jr, e_U^T prod_r Y^L_jr R=(1-V_j)e_U^T with V_j=prod_r(1-L_jr), since Y^2=Y and e_U^T R=0. ### R145. Signed ternary intersection (dual clause selection)
V_j is the indicator of a partial falsifying assignment sigma_j. Repeated identical pins coalesce; opposite pins make a tautological clause (V_j=0). Empty clause has V_j=1. For each partial assignment sigma use E_sigma(x)=1 iff x extends sigma. E_empty=1; E_sigma E_rho=E_(sigma union rho) on compatibility, 0 otherwise. The ternary pin states are unpinned (neutral, both values inhabited), pinned 0, pinned 1; incompatible intersection is bottom, distinct from neutral. Expand F_t=prod_j(1-E_sigma_j). Exact weighted contraction is sum over compatible selected clause subsets S of (-1)^|S| prod_{i forced by union} w_i(sigma_i) prod_{i unforced}(w_i(0)+w_i(1)). This is inclusion-exclusion on the original violation cylinders, not an assumed new intrinsic lower bound. Identical union states can merge with signed coefficients, not independent per-occurrence guard weights. Conflict graph alone is insufficient: the union records repeated same-polarity pins and pays their weights only once.

### R146. Exact sparse frontier
For a supplied clause order, frontier after step j is original guards appearing in both processed and remaining clauses. Initialize D[empty]=product of total weights of never-occurring guards. At each clause, copy each state coefficient to the unselected branch, subtract it into the compatible union state on the selected branch, and drop incompatible branches with an exact contradictory-pin certificate. Forget all guards at their final occurrence, multiplying by the weight of their pinned value or the sum of weights if unpinned. Merge equal remaining keys by addition; coefficient zero only deletes a contribution to this consumer, not the source fibres. At termination D[empty]=P_t. Inductively D is the signed sum over prefix selections with all completed guards contracted; future factors cannot mention forgotten guards, proving the step over any commutative ring. If max frontier width is b, at most 3^b live states and at most twice that many temporary branch contributions per clause. Fixed-width array implementation O(c q 3^b) ring operations and polynomial bookkeeping; sparse dictionary sorting may add log factors. Compare primal 2^b frontier DP on the same cut, which is a better worst-case width bound; dual advantage is sparse reached states or symbolic cancellation, not a superior universal exponent. No requirement to compute an optimal order. Exact component separation, duplicate clauses and subsumed violation cylinders can be simplified with certificates. Reverse direction: each expansion branch determines the chosen source clause occurrences and their intersection, but merged coefficients do not recover those choices; source IDs, signs and independent provenance remain attached. A final scalar cannot recover the complete source.

Exact P_t=sum_h F_t(x_h) prod_l [u^h_l]Q_l(u), where x_h is any canonical assignment of the indicated counts. Compute coefficient arrays by multiplication, O(sum_l |B_l|^2) ring operations; evaluate source on prod_l(|B_l|+1) representatives. Polynomial for fixed block count even without weight symmetry. Reverse orbit members are the original assignments with that count vector, not reconstructed from the contracted coefficient. Original clause example: all positive r-subset clauses on q guards, F=1 iff #zeros<r; P_t=sum_{h=q-r+1}^q [u^h]Q(u). For fixed r>=2, polynomial source size, complete primal interaction graph, nonnilpotent actual G84 transfer algebra, but one-block polynomial contraction. This is a negative control against mandatory frontier/algebra cost, not an intrinsic lower bound.

Core pseudocode:
    normalize each clause to its falsifying partial assignment; skip contradictory pins
    last[i] = last clause containing i
    D = {(): product(sum(w[i]) for absent i)}
    for j,c in enumerate(clauses):
        E = defaultdict(0)
        for s,a in D.items():
            E[s] += a
            if compatible(s,c): E[union(s,c)] -= a
        D = defaultdict(0)
        for s,a in E.items():
            for i with last[i]==j:
                a *= w[i][s[i]] if i in s else sum(w[i])
                erase i from s
            D[s] += a
        reduce coefficients in target ring; remove zero coefficients
    return D.get((),0)

### R150. Joint geometric interface

Product error lies in (2^h) since each child error times the opposite actual/representative value is sufficiently divisible; when a child omitted because its bound meets demand, its replacement zero is also valid. ### R191. Certified original affine extraction
Group original clauses of width 2 or 3 with distinct variable labels by exact sorted scope. A clause on its full scope forbids exactly the assignment with its negative literals set to 1 and positive literals to 0. If all forbidden assignments of one parity p occur in that scope group, their conjunction is exactly XOR(scope)=1-p. Remove only those original clause IDs (duplicates may be removed together); retain other clauses. If both parity sets occur extract both rows, which is contradictory. Original formula F equals residual CNF F_res AND Hx=s over F2, pointwise; forward and reverse proof is equality of the prohibited assignment set. Keep original labels and extraction ledger, do not infer parity from an incomplete set. Empty clauses remain explicit rejection. Discovery is a source scan, constant-size scope pattern enumeration, grouping and linear algebra, not a semantic affine-factor oracle. Scope restricted to widths 2/3 for this implementation, not all affine subrelations. R164 original deterministic compiler equivalence applies if its unique-extension/unit-auxiliary contract is paid; no new compiled-CNF execution. Original R85 P=sum_x w(x) F(x), D=3A-4P, unchanged.

### R192. Three actual source charts in one original order
Use original guard order (not optimal/min-fill primal ordering). Primal chart retains the assignments of processed guards whose last original incident clause is not yet completed, evaluates each clause at its last guard, then forgets dead coordinates. Signed incidence chart retains live satisfied-clause bits, ORs actual positive/negative occurrence masks, rejects an unsatisfied clause at its last guard and forgets its bit. This is per-variable R159 status geometry, not new grouped-region execution. Mixed chart uses residual clauses plus partial syndrome h_k in the original extracted row coordinates (including redundant rows); prune iff s-h_k not in span of suffix columns, with actual binary elimination bases. At terminal require residual status completed and h_n=s. Inconsistency detected by initial suffix membership. Original guard weights charged once; labelled transitions b=0/1 retain original index. Reachable layered graph discovered by actual states, not all ambient assignments. Original clause parsing, mask and suffix-basis construction, bit widths, state dictionary and membership cost charged; one attempted transition is not one equal-cost CPU instruction. Given graph with E edges and N layers, forward/backward weighted/support evaluation O(E+N) ring/Boolean operations plus state storage/bookkeeping. No generic polynomial graph-construction guarantee. A full primal frontier is not mandatory, and a poor fixed order is not a lower bound for optimized primal or other nonlinear algorithms.

A nonempty group assignment has zero-occurs flag z, one-occurs flag o, parity p; exact packet computed by OR of flags and XOR of p with each original bit, storing weighted sum and separately logical support. Compute intersection basis by projecting prefix columns modulo suffix span, eliminating projected columns while tracking actual prefix lifts; kernel relations give exactly U intersect V. ### R209. Arbitrary-rank Weil–Deligne local-factor classification
B_b=1+U+2(U tensor xi^b) by R79 actual Sym^2 dual identity, xi unramified quadratic. Each original assignment x of R78 has unique extension, tag lambda_x=prod z_i^x_i and truth b=F(x), actual WD direct summand B_b tensor eta_lambda. For ANY admissible complex Frobenius-semisimple WD probe V=(rho,N) of any rank, put K0=(ker N)^I and KU=(ker(1 tensor N))^I in U tensor V; A0,AU actual Phi actions. On tensoring by eta_lambda, both inertia and monodromy kernels unchanged and Phi multiplies lambda. Therefore inverse Euler factor of each summand is
 det(1-lambda T A0) det(1-lambda T AU) det(1-(-1)^b lambda T AU)^2.
This is genuine local-factor formula, not an analogy to a Boolean observer. The same direct sum multiplication, with arbitrary probe, shows epsilon(R_F tensor V) independent of F at fixed original tags: changing b replaces TWO copies of W=U tensor eta_lambda tensor V by W tensor xi; the WD unramified twist rule multiplies epsilon by (-1)^(a(W)+dim(W)n(psi)) per copy (reciprocal convention same sign), and square is 1. ### R321. Local-five root number without global functional equation

The primitive Fourier block restricts to U + (U tensor xi), where U is the F5 permutation augmentation, dimension4, xi the unramified quadratic character. This is an actual representation decomposition, not an equality of one scalar probe. With I=affine F5, P=translations, lower filtration I,P,1 from R313, a(U)=4+(5/20)*4=5 and no inertia invariants. For conductor-zero additive psi5, Tate/Deligne unramified twist and duality imply W(U tensor xi)=-W(U), W(U)^2=det U(-1)=1. Therefore W5(V)=-1 independently of the global functional equation. ### R322. Same-object normalized Laurent convolution, with sign and Haar fixed
Let W be the R307 compact-type space, lambda:J->GL(W), g=varpi_K=P tensor I4, A=Lambda_1(g)=-Id in the chosen extended type. Use the left-action tensor convention M_pi=(pi tensor W*)^J=Hom_J(W,pi). Define matrix-valued Hecke kernels H_m supported on g^m J, with H_m(g^m j)=(A^(-m))^t lambda^*(j), where lambda^*(j)=lambda(j)^(-t). Here t is transpose, not conjugation. g normalizes J and A intertwines lambda with its conjugate, hence the kernels are left/right lambda^*-covariant. Fix Haar vol(J)=1; define convolution (H_m*H_n)(x)=integral H_m(y) H_n(y^-1 x) dy. On g^(m+n)J, write y=g^m j; equivariance cancels the two lambda^*(j) factors and gives H_(m+n), with zero elsewhere. H_0 is the type projector identity; H_1 inverse H_-1. The action integral of pi(x) tensor H_m(x) on J-invariants is pi(g^m) tensor (A^(-m))^t, i.e. f -> pi(g^m) f A^(-m). Thus on the canonical type line of pi_alpha it acts as alpha^m, not -alpha^m or alpha^-m. The sign is removed by A, and the inverse convention is explicit. This proves actual convolution identification on the normalizer-supported Laurent subalgebra. With the existing Bushnell-Kutzko maximal simple type intertwining statement I_G(lambda)=Jtilde (R307/R317), all supported double cosets are exactly g^m J, each Hom line one-dimensional by Schur, so this is the full type Hecke algebra at classical theorem authority, not an independently executed p-adic integration or proof of BK. ### R326. Explicit conductor-zero balanced flag and original type functional

Every adjacent root is multiplied by 3, hence psi_F^1 becomes psi_0. det(q)=3^2*7^4*19^6; lattice grades in order (14,9,7,2,0,-5,-7,-12); within each block grades have distinct parity and q splits the original chain. q and q^-1 give the explicit original-coordinate transport. The unramified loop scalar does not change this flag.
R307 scalar kappa and the finite cuspidal rho factor the PS functional. ### R332. Actual finite Cartan support bound, not an exhaustive search
Let K0=GL8(Z3) in the original R306 lattice coordinates, K(c)=1+3^c M8(Z3), and K1=K1(3^20) in standard conductor-zero flag coordinates. The actual right group is q K1 q^-1. For original address g write b=gq=k D h, k,h in K0, D=diag(3^d_i), d_1<=...<=d_8. The type invariant condition uses H_b=Jtilde intersect b K1 b^-1. Since K1 subset K0 and contains K(20), conjugating K(20) by b gives k D K(20) D^-1 k^-1. For any proper parabolic radical N (or conjugate), Lambda^{Jtilde intersect N}=0: restrict c-Ind_{Jtilde}^G Lambda to N, splitting finite-support functions on the discrete Jtilde\G into N-orbits. Each orbit is compact induction from its open stabilizer; the coinvariant summand on identity orbit is Lambda_{Jtilde intersect N}, by summing over quotient cosets. This summand is zero since pi is supercuspidal, and invariants equal coinvariants for the compact stabilizer over C by averaging. The stabilizer is compact since Jtilde intersect N lies in J (det valuation4m on pi^m J; N has det1). Apply to N=k N_i k^-1, where N_i consists of upper-right i-by-(8-i) off-diagonal block and identities elsewhere. If d_(i+1)-d_i>=20, then for every n in N_i intersect K0, D^-1 n D lies in K(20) since cross-block entry valuation increases by d_j-d_r>=20. Consequently Jtilde intersect N subset H_b, contradicting nonzero H_b invariants. Thus adjacent gaps <=19. Central 3I lies in Jtilde (3/pi^2 a unit in K), so normalize d_1=0 without losing any double coset. Then d_8<=133. All actual support has a representative within this finite Cartan range. The R323 coefficient integration level L=3+spread is <=136 on normalized candidates. This is a loose finite bound, not a claim of efficient enumeration or a found coset.

### R333. Covector and type-image reduction
Left factor k may be taken from K(3)\K0 since K(3) subset J acts trivially on Lambda by R323. Right factor h modulo K1 can be represented by primitive row v=e8^* h^-1 mod3^20; every primitive row extends to an invertible matrix over Z3, and equal rows mod3^20 give the same right K1 coset. Retain actual h or a chosen lift and row identity; no arbitrary identification of different original addresses by scalar character. For j in H_b, det valuation is zero so j is in J. With z=D^-1 k^-1 j k D, membership is exactly z integral and v z=v mod3^20 (det unit automatic). Set M=max(3,20+spread)<=153. Then K(M) subset H_b and acts trivially on type: entrywise conjugation loses at most spread. Hence projector on invariants is finite average of Lambda over H_b/K(M). Better: project H_b to G_b=image(H_b in J/K(3)); all fibres equal cardinality and Lambda factors through J/K(3), so projector is exactly |G_b|^-1 sum_{jbar in G_b} Lambda(jbar) and rank is average of its character. H_b lies in compact J, not uniformizer shells. All linear constraints on j entries can be solved as an additive module before enforcing invertibility: J congruences in R307 and A-integrality, z integral and v(z-I)=0 mod3^20; write j=I+Y, clear denominators by explicit powers of3. Project module solutions to Y mod27 with exact lift feasibility over finite Z/3^M; invertibility depends only mod3 and the A-unit and GL4 residue conditions. Use actual k lift and v; if only a row representative mod3^20 is stored, integrality of z makes its choice irrelevant. This gives a finite original-coordinate image computation by modular linear algebra instead of enumerating full GL7 quotient at L=136. No assertion that every element of the projected additive module is invertible, or that counting without the actual character gives invariant rank. The finite image solver and full R307 character trace/projector execution remain unpaid; the toy projection test is not the rank8 solver. Candidate generation bounded by d tuples, k residue and primitive row; duplicates may occur and should be deduped by actual double-coset equivalence if enumerating. Newform existence/uniqueness guarantees rank one at precisely one double coset (not precisely one candidate tuple).

### R334. Exact whole-family block-shear exclusion using actual beta
Use interleaved original (1,pi) blocks, a permutation of R306, and Q_0=X diag(3^7,3^6) from R326. In standard flag coordinates put u(a)=I+a E12 in the first block (a in Q3). Conjugating E21 gives
T(a)=Q_0 u(a) E21 u(a)^-1 Q_0^-1=[[a+2/9,(9a+2)^2/21],[-7/27,-a-2/9]]
and Tr(beta T(a))/3=-(243a^2-81a+19)/243.
Choose ell=4 when v3(a)>=-2 (including a=0), ell=-2 v3(a)-1 otherwise. Then Y=3^ell T lies in P_A^2, the entrywise thresholds [1,2;1,1]. Proof: for first case the lower-left requires ell>=4 and all other thresholds hold; the trace numerator is a 3-adic unit. For v3(a)=s<=-3, the valuations of diagonal, upper-right, lower-left are s,2s+3,-3 respectively; ell=-2s-1 suffices, and 243a^2 has unique smallest valuation 5+2s in trace numerator, giving trace valuation 2s before scaling. Thus v3(Tr(beta Y)/3)=-1 in both cases. Y^2=0, 1+Y in U_A^2 with nontrivial cubic scalar tau. For g=q u q^-1 with u block diagonal upper shears (arbitrary other blocks), 1+Y is the conjugate by gq of I+3^ell E21 (inside K1 because ell>=0 and row2 is not row8). Therefore H_b invariants vanish. In particular identity address excluded: Y=[[18,108/7],[-21,-18]], exponent -19/3. This is a general exact valuation argument over Q3, not extrapolation from rational samples. For full upper unitriangular u, first 2x2 diagonal block still equals u(a); the same scaled conjugate E21 is nilpotent and has the same trace because beta block diagonal and u block upper triangular. If every cross-block entry satisfies the P_A^2 thresholds, it is still a character obstruction. Hence any U_q supporting representative must have nontrivial cross-block coupling that violates at least one threshold for this first-block obstruction. Failure of this obstruction does NOT establish support. Retain coupled blocks and signed nontrivial character, do not use independent per-block diagonal search. Other root tests and full J1-kappa image necessary; no found support address.

### R346. Actual covector forces finite mirabolic subgroup on every Whittaker summand
Use interleaved original (1,pi) block coordinates and the actual R342 cyclic b, R326 q and g0=b q^-1. In the R343 unnormalized f_alpha with unit v_minus=rho(d0)v0, the R326 evaluation W_f(1) is the finite sum over left (U_q intersect Jtilde)-cosets in U_q of psi_q(u)^-1 ell_type(f_alpha(u)). Compactness follows because determinant valuation fixes the uniformizer shell m in support and J and right K1 are compact. v3(det b)=-54, v3(det q)=2, hence det(g0) valuation -56 and m=14 for every contributing u (det u=1). In q coordinates write u=R j b k with R=q^-1 pi^14, j in J, k in K1(3^20). The last row of u is e8*, so e8* R j = e8* b^-1 + 3^20 Z3^8 b^-1. Put r=e8*R and c=e8*b^-1. Exact r has only last two entries nonzero with valuations (13,16); componentwise error precision is (20,21,20,19,18,20,17,16). Reducing the even-indexed original 1-coordinate entries (zero-based indices 0,2,4,6) after division by r[6] gives c/r[6] residues (0,0,0,1). J lies in A and its reduction to GL4(F3) uses the original centralizer C block, so r[7] term vanishes mod3 after dividing by r[6]. Thus every contributing j has last residue row exactly e4*, i.e. its finite image lies in the actual mirabolic P4(F3). This is not an approximation of the original support. Retain j and original coset witness.
Since d0=diag(-1,1,-1,1) also lies in P4, the R293 orthonormal mirabolic induced basis implies ell_type(lambda(j) v_minus)=kappa(j) B_rho(jbar d0); B_rho on P4 is zero outside U4 and is the standard cubic character on U4. Hence this value is zero or a cubic root of unity. This eliminates all fifth-root dependence from the Whittaker normalization WITHOUT evaluating the general 416-dimensional finite action or changing rho. Original signed phase and actual subgroup membership retained. The factor Lambda_alpha(pi^14)=alpha^14 (since (-1)^14=1) is common. Therefore W_f_alpha(1)=alpha^14 S, with S a finite sum of roots of 3-power order, no denominators from finite Bessel averaging. Coarse bound min v3(R)=0, min v3(b)=-18 and integral j,k imply all standard u entries in 3^-18 Z3, so S in Z[zeta_(3^18)]. No actual sum evaluated.

### R347. Diagonal unit symmetry removes all higher ternary phases
Let a in 1+3 Z3 and D(a)=diag(a^7,a^6,...,a,1) in standard flag coordinates. This lies in K1 and normalizes U. For each original block let E_i=Q_i diag(1,0) Q_i^-1. Exact E_i belongs to A, upper-right entry divisible by3, Tr(beta_i E_i)=0, Tr(beta_i(1-E_i))=-1. Consequently q D(a) q^-1-I lies in 3A and its scalar phase Tr(beta(qDq^-1-I))/3 equals -sum_(i=0..3)(a^(6-2i)-1)/3. Writing a=1+3z, every polynomial coefficient is divisible by3 (linear coefficient -12). Thus q D(a) q^-1 acts trivially in lambda. It normalizes the left compact U_q intersection and conjugation gives a permutation of contributing cosets, with the finite coefficient unchanged, since f is right K1-fixed and left type covariant. Simple-root coordinates scale by a, and hence the additive psi phase transforms by the cyclotomic Galois automorphism zeta_(3^18)->zeta_(3^18)^a. Cubic type phases fixed. Thus S fixed by Gal(Q(zeta_(3^18))/Q(zeta3)), and because it is integral, S belongs to Z[zeta3]. Equivalently average over a mod3^18 with a=1 mod3: individual additive terms vanish unless simple-root sum has valuation >=-1, in which case the cubic phase survives. This projection preserves the actual signed sum, not the unsigned number of source cosets; record original cancelled and surviving fibres if implementing BIDI. No assumption that each individual summand already has conductor one.

### R348. Exact six-unit phase residual and minimal sufficient signed-count precision
Independently recomputed R327 pairing volumes vH=49, vU=-77 from the actual Q_i matrices by reversing constraint order and taking minimal valuations of maximal minors, hence C=3^-126/p7. R344 support index I_b=3^222 p7 retained at its prior arithmetic receipt (not independently recomputed this tranche). Under declared R326/R327 Whittaker functional/coset and invariant pairing convention, |S|^2=C I_b=3^96 at alpha=1. With R347 S=A+B zeta3 in Z[zeta3], norm A^2-AB+B^2=3^96. Elementary division by 1-zeta3 (norm3) is possible iff A+B=0 mod3; dividing 96 times leaves norm1, whose six solutions are the Eisenstein units. Since (1-zeta3)^2=-3 zeta3, (1-zeta3)^96=3^48. Therefore W_f_alpha(1)=alpha^14*3^48*epsilon, epsilon in {+/-1,+/-zeta3,+/-zeta3^2}, using the exact fixed R343 choice v_minus and R326 functional. Nonunitary alpha extension via determinant shell formula, not a positive invariant norm claim for nonunitary cases. This is a six-candidate normalization, NOT actual evaluation of epsilon.
More explicitly average as R347; let N_i count original left cosets with nonzero Bessel coefficient and surviving additive phase, total cubic exponent i including the negative psi exponent and kappa/Bessel sign. The exponent is invariant on each left coset by Whittaker covariance; to apply the individual additive survival test one must choose representatives compatibly with the averaging, or equivalently count on a common finite finer quotient with equal Haar weights and divide by its index. In fact U intersect J may have nontrivial cubic or higher additive phase, so survival of a chosen representative is not automatically a well-defined coset condition; use a finite diagonal-stable finer quotient on which both factors separately descend and retain exact multiplicities. On the original quotient the total phase is invariant; diagonal symmetry implies it is in the cubic subgroup only after orbit averaging, not a priori. The sharper fact here is psi on U intersect J has cubic values by the R326 kappa times finite residue character (on the functional line); hence v3(simple-root sum)>=-1 there, so additive denominator <=3 is invariant on original left cosets, and averaging indeed yields original integer counts N_i. Then S=(N0-N2)+(N1-N2) zeta3. To distinguish the six units it suffices to compute both signed differences mod3^49: divide their known factor3^48 and reduce mod3; six unit pairs are distinct in F3^2. No need exact counts or reconstruct full original coset enumeration. Keep occupied zero and all original count fibres independent of reduced residue, following Section66 donor distinction between projected witness and signed consumer. ### R349. Direct normalized-newvector forward Rankin-Selberg integral
Use essential Whittaker newform theorem (existing R316 Matringe source) for original supercuspidals, normalized W_alpha(1)=W_beta^vee(1)=1 with opposite generic characters. On mirabolic P8 each W restricts to the canonical compact Kirillov support U7 GL7(Z3) and unit character values there. Choose standard conductor-zero row test Phi=1_(e8*+3^20 Z3^8). If e8*g in this row coset, choose k in K1 with last row e8*g (complete with first seven identity rows, determinant unit), so g k^-1 in P8. Right K1 invariance then makes the product of the two Whittaker functions supported precisely on U8 K1, with product 1 and |det g|=1. Normalize Haar vol(GL8(Z3))=vol(U8(Z3))=1. Because U8 intersect K1=U8(Z3), the quotient mass of this support is vol(K1)=1/(3^160-3^152)=3^-160/(1-3^-8), via primitive row count. Thus direct forward integral Z_new(s)=3^-160/(1-3^-8), independent of s and alpha/beta. This analytic evaluation uses the existing newform theorem, not a numerical smooth-representation execution nor an evaluation of epsilon in R348. Fourier transform is 3^-160 psi_0(e8*x) times indicator of 3^-20 Z3^8 (sign follows declared Fourier convention). Independent dual integral on actual normalized compact-induction representation still open. R328 gives predicted dual via functional equation, not independent evaluation: multiply Z_new by r^27*3^(-108(s-1/2))*(1-r*3^-4s)/(1-r^-1*3^-4(1-s)), r=alpha/beta, with central sign1 as in R328. Directly derive and compare instead of assuming this prediction. Note test function differs from centered lattice of R328, do not identify integrals or Haar masses without transport.

## 2026-11-25 — R351–R353: original unipotent lift and independent simple-root Fourier gates

### R351. Reverse-solve an original unipotent representative, not a new Cartan search
Let R=q^-1 pi^14 in the actual interleaved source coordinates, and d0=diag(-1,1,-1,1) in GL4(F3). A summand has nonzero type coefficient precisely when its residue lies in U4 d0^-1 (R346). Fix k=1 and solve j=R^-1 u b^-1 for upper-unipotent u. Set u=I+3^-18 sum_{i<j} x_ij E_ij with x_ij integral. The J conditions are affine: integral entries, equality of the two diagonal entries in each 2-by-2 block mod3, upper-right plus 3 times lower-left zero mod9, and Cbar upper triangular with diagonal d0. Unit determinant follows from the residue. Valuation-pivot affine elimination on these 28 coordinates produced the exact rational u in scripts/check_langlands_root_filter.py. Its Cbar is [[2,0,2,2],[0,1,0,0],[0,0,2,2],[0,0,0,1]], so Cbar*d0 is upper unipotent. Exact j membership, determinant valuation zero, and R j b=u are verified independently. The adjacent entries are (4,56/3,26/3,70/3,14/3,71/3,0) and their sum is 83. This is a genuine original unipotent representative with nonzero summand, not a normalized coefficient. All k=1 witnesses are the same left (U intersect q^-1 J q) coset, since R normalizes the conjugated J. No inference that this coset alone determines the Whittaker phase.

### R352. Seven independent diagonal ratios replace the common-scale filter
For t_i in 1+3 Z3, i=1,...,7, let a_i=product_{j=i}^7 t_j and a_8=1, D=diag(a_i), so D lies in K1 and conjugation scales each adjacent coordinate u_i=u_(i,i+1) independently by t_i. R347 actual projectors E_i in A and their traces 0,-1 imply qDq^-1 is in U_A^2 with scalar action kappa=psi_0(-sum_{i even}(a_i-1)/3). Here the sum includes i=8 with zero contribution. Write t_i=1+3z_i. Modulo 3 in the cubic exponent, kappa= zeta3^(-sum m_i z_i), m_i=floor(i/2), since each t_i occurs in exactly m_i of the even-indexed a_j. This uses the original scalar action, not a trivial torus assumption. On the original left coset sum, conjugation by D permutes cosets and support, by left J covariance and right K1 invariance. For F(u)=psi_0(-sum u_i) ell_type(f(q u q^-1)), the multiplier is psi_0(-sum z_i(3u_i+m_i/3)). Average independently over the seven z_i, with sufficient finite precision (R346 bounds u_i in 3^-18 Z3). Orthogonality annihilates every term unless ALL seven conditions hold:
    3 u_i + m_i/3 in Z3, equivalently u_i in -m_i/9 + (1/3)Z3.
These conditions descend to the actual left coset: for h in U intersect q^-1 J q, psi(h) is its type Whittaker character; conjugation by the scalar qDq^-1 preserves that character. Independent ratios then force each adjacent h_i in (1/3)Z3. Adjacent coordinates add under left multiplication, so the seven tests are representative-independent. The R351 k=1 representative fails four tests (i=2,3,4,5); its full diagonal-averaged contribution vanishes. In particular the unipotent lift by itself is not a phase computation. Write each surviving root u_i=-m_i/9+x_i/3; since sum m_i=12, sum u_i=(sum x_i-4)/3. Thus the surviving additive phase is zeta3^(4-sum x_i) and the type phase remains the actual kappa times finite U4 character of Cbar*d0. This refines R348: the full signed count is now a count on seven explicit affine root fibres, with no higher cyclotomic arithmetic, and original coset identities and signs preserved. Norm 3^96 still conditional on R327 pairing; it does not identify the remaining Eisenstein unit.

### R353. Exact residual affine lifting and remaining weld
For a fixed u, let K=k^-1=I+L, with L integral and bottom row in 3^20 Z3^8. Then j=R^-1 u K b^-1 is affine in the 64 K coordinates. Impose the same original J and U4*d0 residue constraints as R351. Every solution has det j a unit, and det(R b) valuation zero and det u=1, so det K is automatically a unit; K really is in K1. Conversely any contributing u supplies such a K. This gives an exact linear fibre feasibility test with original lifts for any u, not a claim that joint u,K elimination is affine or that counting is cheap. For two lifts at the same u their difference in J is controlled by the actual R343 supporting stabilizer; invariance of v_minus proves identical summand phase. The R348 signed count can therefore use one lift per original left U coset, not multiply by the number of K lifts. Still need compute the two signed residues modulo 3^49 on the actual narrowed quotient, with exact multiplicity and zero/residual ledger. No positive surviving representative is claimed in this cut. A3 translated-row dual integral, B3b geometric intertwiners, C1 monodromic globalization and C2 categorical descent remain open; do not infer them from this scalar filter.

### R354. Correct R352's conductor-zero character, superseding the shifted gates
R326 defines psi_F^1(x)=psi_0(x/3) with psi_0 trivial on Z3. R347 correctly uses the trace divided by 3. For independent ratios t_i=1+3z_i and D=diag(a_i), a_i=product_{j>=i} t_j, a_8=1, the same actual block projectors give
    kappa(qDq^-1)=psi_0(-sum_{i even}(a_i-1)/3)=1.
R352 mistakenly treated this integral argument as a cubic exponent. Thus its shifted gates, negative-control cancellation and final phase formula are withdrawn; its checker checked formal exponent arithmetic, not the actual conductor-zero character. Conjugation preserves each coset and type-coefficient support as a permutation of the quotient; its effect on summands is only psi_0(-3 sum_i z_i u_i). Independent finite Fourier averaging kills terms unless all u_i in (1/3)Z3. These tests descend by the actual stabilizer root image below. R351's original witness (4,56/3,26/3,70/3,14/3,71/3,0) passes every corrected gate. Its adjacent sum is 83, hence additive phase 1. Independently evaluated the R341 kappa determinant lookup and trace and actual U4 Bessel coefficient of jbar*d0: their product is exactly 1. Thus this is a surviving coset of phase +1 at alpha=1 (not the full signed count).

### R355. Remove all seven simple roots by an actual original left-stabilizer lift
In standard q coordinates write H=U intersect q^-1 J q. The additive increment lattice of J before invertibility is blockwise O_K+3A with basis I2, P, diag(3,0), 9E12. For upper-unipotent h the determinant and residue invertibility are automatic; the actual H consists of I plus that lattice intersected with strictly upper triangular coordinates after conjugation by q. Compute each of the 16 independent block intersections via valuation-pivot elimination of forbidden lower and diagonal entries. Their volume exponents in order are (-1,-11,-25,-39;0,-1,-11,-25;0,0,-1,-11;0,0,0,-1), total -126, hence vol(H)=3^126 for product upper-entry Haar. Each adjacent root is in a different original block (four diagonal blocks and three adjacent off-diagonal blocks). The exact projected ideal for each is 3^-1 Z3. Consequently root:H -> (3^-1 Z3)^7 is a surjective group homomorphism (adjacent entries add). Let U^0=ker(root:U->F^7), H^0=H intersect U^0. Any surviving u has a constructive h in H with root(h)=root(u), by independently choosing the corresponding valuation-minimal block lift and summing increments; w=h^-1 u lies in U^0. Conversely two root-free representatives of the same H-left coset differ by H^0. Thus H^0\U^0 bijects with the corrected surviving H-left cosets. Fubini in upper coordinates gives vol(H^0)=3^119, since projected root volume is 3^7. Covariance preserves the entire signed summand, not simply its support. Recovered an explicit root-free version of the R351 witness by this lift, still with k=1 and j'=R^-1 w b^-1 in J with finite residue in U4*d0; min entry valuation -15, phase +1. No higher cyclotomic phase remains; all simple-root coordinates now exactly zero, and the residual type-character exponent alone labels the signed fibres. Actual residual dimension 21 instead of 28, no auxiliary original roots enumerated.

For each root-free w let K=k^-1 in K1, j=R^-1 w K b^-1. Restrict Cbar(j) to U4*d0 and impose the original J congruences. If K0 is a solution, the complete fibre is K0 (K1 intersect b^-1 J b). Indeed j0^-1 j=b K0^-1 K b^-1 has image in U4; conversely the R343 supporting stabilizer image is exactly U4 so the whole intersection preserves the required residue coset and vector. The summand phase is independent of K by actual stabilizer invariance. R344 additive intersection mass 3^-382 (conjugation by b preserves additive matrix measure, left multiplication by a unit has modulus1), retained at prior arithmetic receipt. Thus every admitted w has the SAME right-lift mass, and every H^0-left coset has mass 3^119. The total pair mass per contributing coset is 3^-263. This is exact overlap correction, not a forest lower bound; distinct original left cosets counted once and all right witnesses recoverable via K0 and the stabilizer. Use the uniform R346 support denominator bound w entries in 3^-18 Z3, with all simple roots zero. Write w=I+3^-18 X, X strictly above first superdiagonal (21 entries), K=I+diag(1,1,1,1,1,1,1,3^20)Y (64 entries). Exact min valuations of R^-1 and b^-1 are -13,-4. Thus the finite box is exact, not a numerical truncation. For each phase i let C_i count feasible (X,Y) cells mod3^38, and A_i count just X cells admitting a lift of phase i. K-cell mass is 3^(-160-64M); w-cell mass is 3^(378-21M). The uniform right fibre has 3^(64M-222)=3^2210 cells. Each root-free original left coset has 3^(21M-259)=3^539 w-cells. Hence
    C_i = 3^2210 A_i, A_i = 3^539 N_i, C_i = 3^2749 N_i.
S=(N0-N2)+(N1-N2)zeta3. To recover the unit under the prior norm convention one needs only A0-A2 and A1-A2 modulo 3^588 (or pair differences modulo 3^2798), then divide by the exact common power and reduce modulo 3^49 as in R348. No need to enumerate Y: R353 affine solver can return one K and exact phase per X, with the uniform multiplicity proof above. ### R357. Reverse the original pair integral and eliminate all root-free unipotent variables
Let U^0 be the root-free unipotent group, and J_d the original affine type subset j in J with Cbar in U4*d0. On this subset the actual phase p(j) is the product of R307 kappa and the R346 finite Bessel character; root-free additive phase is 1. For V=R j b, det V is a 3-adic unit. We seek w in U^0, K in K1(3^20) with V=w K. Set w^-1=I+Z, Z strictly above the first superdiagonal. Then
    K_i=V_i+sum_(l>=i+2) Z_il V_l.
The rows of Z are independent unknowns. Thus the necessary and sufficient conditions are the original last row V_8=e8 mod3^20 and, for i=1,...,7, V_i in Z3^8+L_i, L_i=span_F(V_(i+2),...,V_8). Compute a saturated quotient D_i by integral unimodular column operations on the lower-row matrix, so L_i D_i=0 and the columns D_i extend to a Z3 basis. The test is exactly V_i D_i integral. For backward reconstruction select a unit maximal minor of D_i and an integral y_i with y_i D_i=V_i D_i; solve y_i-V_i in original L_i row basis, retaining every Z_il. Then K=(I+Z)V is integral and unit determinant, and its last row is correct, so K really lies in K1. No simultaneous nonlinear row-choice problem remains. Implemented exact valuation pivots and witness reconstruction. This does not equate the quotient spaces of distinct lower-row inputs.

For fixed admitted j, if w is one lift, all lifts are exactly w U^0(Z3): w'K'=wK implies w^-1 w'=K K'^-1 in K1 intersect U^0, and conversely every such right translate gives a lift. This fibre has upper-coordinate Haar mass 1; it carries the same phase p(j). For each w, the change K -> j=R^-1 w K b^-1 has additive matrix Jacobian |det(R b)|_3^-8=1, using det w=1. Therefore Fubini on the compact supported pair integral from R356 gives the exact reversed normalization
    S=3^263 integral_(j in J_d) 1_row(j) p(j) dj,
where dj is additive 64-entry Haar measure and 1_row is the seven saturated quotient tests plus bottom covector. This is not a new normalization convention: the factor 3^263 is precisely the prior pair mass inverse, conditional on R344/R355 volumes. ### R360. Reverse block Gaussian factorization on the original order

Let O be the actual two-by-two order with Z3 basis I2,P,diag(3,0),9E12 (R355). The residue map c:O -> F3 sends M to M11 mod3 and is a ring homomorphism. An element with c nonzero is a unit in O, by the determinant and adjugate formula. For j in J_d, its four-by-four block residue is U4*d0. Eliminate from the bottom-right block upward: for a pivot d, partition the matrix as [[m,x],[y,d]], take a=x d^-1, ell=d^-1 y and Schur complement m-a d ell. The pivot is a unit and the residual block residue retains its upper-triangular form and original diagonal signs (lower y has zero residue). Iteration gives unique
    j=A D L,
where A is block upper unitriangular over O, D is block diagonal with c(D_ii)=d0_i, and L is block lower unitriangular with c(L_ij)=0 below diagonal. BIDI original matrix recovered by multiplication; every such triple gives j in J_d. No compulsory dense GL4 representation. At each pivot the changes of x,y to a,ell have additive Jacobian one because det d is a unit, and the Schur complement is a translation. Thus product additive Haar is preserved. The six A blocks have mass 3^-18, the four D and six L blocks have mass 3^-40 (each prescribed c costs one additional power of 3), total 3^-58, exactly the original J_d mass. Equivalently B0=D L is lower block triangular with the same product measure; each below-diagonal block is in the residue-zero ideal, since D_i acts by a unit.

Thus the three root residues t are exactly the three adjacent c(A_ij), and every t in F3^3 has Haar mass 3^-21 in A. Explicit section A(t) has adjacent blocks t_i I2, other strict upper blocks zero. The scalar kappa of A and L is trivial: factor them into elementary off-diagonal block unipotents, choose centralizer lifts of determinant one, and their radical trace is zero. Finite Bessel on j*d0 is the upper-unipotent character on Abar. Hence p(j)=kappa(D) zeta3^(sum t). This is the original signed phase, not an unsigned mass.

For V0=R B0 b, solve V0=u K with u in full U (not U^0), K in K1. Use R357's saturated row quotients with suffix starting at i+1 rather than i+2. For V0 in U K1, any g in U satisfies g V0 in U^0 K1 iff root(g)+root(u) belongs to Z3^7: necessity follows from the lift ambiguity; sufficiency by splitting the integral simple roots off using an element of U(Z3), which lies in K1. ### R373. Literal minuscule modifications on the original rank-eight supporting lattice
Use actual R342 b, L=b Z3^8. For k=0..8, take L' with 3L subset L' subset L and length(L/L')=k. Reduction modulo 3 gives W=L'/3L of dimension 8-k in L/3L, with inverse W -> b(preimage of W under reduction). BIDI original W and L' recovered, not just determinant. Choose a pivot basis of W (e.g. unique RREF rows transposed); let C be the integral column matrix of lifted W generators followed by 3 times nonpivot standard vectors. Its determinant is +/-3^k and D=3 C^-1 is integral. L'=b C Z3^8, b C D=3b exactly, also D C=3I. Reverse complement is the actual sublattice 3L in L', index 3^(8-k); the marked chart and scale remain retained, not identified with identity. Basis changes act via GL8(Z3) with corresponding D inverse-basis change. This is the rank-eight analogue of the Paper9 original dual correction, using rational 3 rather than ramified pi, since the lattice is over Q3. Counts k=0..8 are Gaussian binomials [8 choose k]_3:
1,3280,896260,25095280,75913222,25095280,896260,3280,1.
No raw rank-eight Grassmannian enumeration needed. These are actual finite Q3-lattice modifications at the chosen carrier, i.e. hyperspecial spherical lattice geometry. They are not an identification of the compact ramified J-type with a spherical representation, nor the B_dR^+ Hecke stack, nor a construction of an eigensheaf.

### R374. Exact parabolic fibre and normalized finite constant term
Choose an indexed splitting L=L_A direct_sum L_B of ranks r,s=8-r in original b-coordinates (not an invariant proper splitting of irreducible V_alpha). For W subset F3^r direct_sum F3^s, retain X=W intersect F3^r and Y=pr_B W of dimensions a=r-i,b'=s-j; i+j=k. For each fixed pair X,Y, the fine fibre is exactly Hom_F3(Y,F3^r/X): an element y in Y lifts to (x,y), and x mod X is unique; conversely each linear graph map f determines W={(x,y): y in Y, x mod X=f(y)}. This gives actual original reopening including every extension direction, not an arbitrary assignment of weights. The fibre has q^{(s-j)i} elements at q=3. Consequently [8 choose k]_q=sum_(i+j=k) [r choose i]_q [s choose j]_q q^{i(s-j)}, standard q-Vandermonde with this orientation.
On the finite minuscule correspondence, group the summed upper-extension fibre by the two Levi modifications. With normalized operators scaled by q^{-k(8-k)/2}, q^{-i(r-i)/2}, q^{-j(s-j)/2}, respectively, and the upper parabolic convention delta_P(diag(A,B))=|det A|^s |det B|^-r=q^{-is+jr}, the exact coefficient is
q^{i(s-j)-k(8-k)/2+i(r-i)/2+j(s-j)/2+(-is+jr)/2}=1.
Thus the normalized finite parabolic pushforward satisfies CT_norm(T_k)=sum_(i+j=k) T_i^A tensor T_j^B in this convention. Opposite parabolic orientation reverses graph count and modulus consistently. This is the lattice/finite spherical constant-term identity; no new theorem on parabolic sheaf functors or on arbitrary ramified compact-type action. The normalized exterior identity on a direct-sum spectral parameter is wedge^k(V_A direct_sum V_B)=direct_sum_(i+j=k) wedge^i V_A tensor wedge^j V_B; preserve the original wedge permutation signs and actual maps. Do not insert an invariant proper Levi splitting for the original irreducible V_alpha. In particular R329 has V_alpha^I=0 but dim((wedge^2 V_alpha)^I)=2; taking inertia invariants before exterior power would erase actual data. The finite correspondence and normalization are reusable inputs for B3b/C2, with the geometric pull-push/sheaf and functorial compatibility remaining distinct.

### R376. Actual object and degree-resolved Hecke maps by existing spectral action
Fix an algebraically closed characteristic-zero l-adic coefficient field, l != 3, alpha nonzero in the coefficient field, and original continuous irreducible Weil representation V_alpha from R268/R317/R339, with the same reciprocity convention and original conductor-zero Whittaker datum (convert psi to coefficient field by chosen compatible root-of-unity realization). Choose square root of q=3 for normalized Hecke functors. The original 8 weight characters are distinct, inertia involution 11 and Frobenius 13 act transitively on their labels; hence irreducibility and centralizer G_m. Use ALB Definition 7.3 and Proposition 7.4: at the residual gerbe of V_alpha let k_reg be its regular representation, decomposed as direct sum k_d of all integral scalar weights d. Define Aut_alpha=k_reg star W_psi using the Fargues–Scholze spectral action, W_psi the compactly induced Whittaker representation !-extended from the trivial bundle stratum. This is an actual geometric construction in the cited formalism, not the finite lattice correspondence or a new abstract postulate in a local proof module. ALB Lemma 7.7 supplies support of k_d star W_psi on degree d. Theorem 1.2 supplies nonvanishing, semistable support and j_d^* Aut_alpha = F_{JL_d(pi_alpha)} in degree zero on every basic stratum, with pi_alpha the original GL8 LLC representative and JL_d generalized local JL. No inference from just eigenvalue dimensions; source theorem includes actual natural Hecke isomorphisms.
For positive-degree convention T_k attached to wedge^k std, the scalar weight is k, and restricting the actual Hecke functor to degree d+k gives
  j_{d+k}^* T_k(j_{d,!} F_{JL_d(pi_alpha)}) ≅ wedge^k V_alpha tensor F_{JL_(d+k)(pi_alpha)}.
Use the chosen normalized functor and Weil action, opposite convention uses dual and reverses degree. This follows from ALB's eigensheaf isomorphism and the degree decomposition (each component of Aut is extended from the unique basic stratum there; support excludes nonbasic). Coherent maps on all weights are the natural maps in Proposition 7.4, not arbitrary scalar isomorphisms chosen after comparing dimensions. The statement is at external theorem authority, not a direct geometric sheaf calculation or pointwise intertwiner matrix on a chosen type basis. At d=0 this recovers the original split representation, not an identification with all inner form representation spaces.

### R377. Original basic isocrystals, determinant twist and signed inverse transport
For every integer degree d let g=gcd(8,d), m=8/g, a=d/g. Use the positive valuation isocrystal convention with sigma the arithmetic Frobenius on the completed unramified field. Let C_(m,a) send basis e_i to e_(i+1) for i<m-1 and e_(m-1) to 3^a e_0. The original basic isocrystal is represented by B_d=diag(C_(m,a),...,C_(m,a)) with g copies, semilinear operator B_d sigma. Rational coefficients are sigma-fixed; B_d^m=3^a I and v3 det B_d=d. By classical Dieudonne–Manin classification it is basic slope d/8. The sigma-centralizer is GL_g(D) where D is the division endomorphism algebra of the simple slope a/m isocrystal. Its degree is m; the explicit sign convention can be fixed by D's generators: for x in the unramified degree m subfield, diag(x,sigma(x),...,sigma^(m-1)(x)) satisfies X C=C sigma(X), C also satisfies it, C X(x) C^-1=X(sigma^-1 x) and C^m=3^a. Thus with arithmetic sigma the cyclic algebra has generator conjugating by sigma^-1 (Brauer invariant opposite to a/m if invariant uses arithmetic sigma as positive generator). Define D by this literal algebra, not by an ambiguous sign label; opposite convention for bundle/isocrystal swaps consistently. For coprime a,m it is division by the cyclic algebra classification, and g copies give matrix algebra, dimension64. The two-sided degree lattice is Z, not Z/8; d mod8 describes the pattern of ranks 8,1,2,1,4,1,2,1 and division degrees 1,8,4,8,2,8,4,8 only. Explicit G=diag(3^-i) on each simple block obeys G(3 C_(m,a))G^-1=C_(m,a+m), proving the original rank-eight determinant/line-bundle degree-eight transport with scale retained. This is distinct from the ramified uniformizer pi in R322.
Retain ordered primitive weights 1,2,4,7,8,11,13,14 and R339 signed exterior basis. For each subset I, J=I^c, exact complement pairing e_I wedge e_J=sign(I,J)e_all gives wedge^(8-k)V ≅ det(V) tensor (wedge^k V)^vee, including translations, involution, Frobenius wrap powers and wedge signs. det(V_alpha) has unramified value alpha^2 on the original Frobenius (F_alpha^4=alpha I). Thus T_det is invertible with inverse T_det^-1; complement Hecke is det-twisted dual, not an inverse of arbitrary minuscule Hecke: on the eigenobject k in 1..7 has multiplicity binomial(8,k)>1. In contrast scalar-weight line actions below really are invertible. This preserves the original zero/occupied and signed exterior modes (inertia invariants dims 1,0,2,0,5,0,2,0,1). No replacement of V_alpha by its inertia invariants.

### R378. Reuse actual localized categorical equivalence and local shtuka computation (Zou)
Zou arXiv:2504.06499 Lemma 2.7: Langlands–Shahidi type for semisimple GL_n parameter with irreducible summands is equivalent to no repeated summands and no distinct pair differing by Tate twist (1). Since V_alpha is irreducible, the condition is vacuous, with full GL8 cuspidal support and stabilizer G_m. Hence Theorem 1.3/6.2 gives the IndPerf-linear equivalence on the LS locus (nilpotent singular support zero there); Theorem 7.3 supplies t-exactness for the specified perverse t-structure; Theorems 8.1,8.6 and 8.7 give localized stratum decomposition, equality of renormalized extensions and actual eigensheaf. This supersedes an open abstract categorical existence/adjunction claim for this irreducible family, not a proof of all CLLC. In the formal neighborhood of V_alpha use Zou Lemma 2.15's line objects O(k,V_alpha) pulled back from scalar characters of B G_m. Tensor product O(k)⊗O(l)=O(k+l), O(k)⊗O(-k)=O(0). Via the spectral action their degree-shift functors are mutually inverse equivalences on this localized basic family; on the residual gerbe fibre, T_{wedge^k} = (wedge^k V_alpha) tensor (O(k) star -) with full Weil action. In a formal family the coefficient local system varies, not a constant V_alpha. This gives the normalized Hecke map and its inverse line transport, unit/counit from tensor-line evaluation and coevaluation (triangle identities from invertibility). It does NOT give a chosen coordinate matrix on a compact-type basis; explicit comparison to R322 type generators, Whittaker normalization and inner-form modules still needed.
Zou Theorem 8.10 computes the actual basic local-shtuka cohomology after tensoring against JL(pi_alpha), in its normalization, as pi_alpha tensor (r_mu circ V_alpha tensor |.|^(-h/2))[h], h=dim Sht. For GL8 minuscule mu=(1^k,0^(8-k)), basic b with matching degree and h=k(8-k), r_mu=wedge^k std in the convention in which positive degree adds k (if convention is opposite dualize consistently). This is a same-parameter local geometric cohomology comparison at theorem authority, with all original rank8 exterior data specified; no independent computation of RΓ_c or direct matching of finite lattice charts with B_dR^+ geometric stalks. Finite spherical lattice cardinalities from R373 are not the geometric cohomological multiplicities.

### R379. Proper parabolic Eisenstein boundary and exact nonzero-monodromy exclusion
HHS arXiv:2409.07363 Theorem 1.2.1 and pp2–3 define the actual stack maps Bun_M <-q- Bun_P -p-> Bun_G and invertible normalization IC with q^! = q^* tensor IC^2. With the paper's six-functor/coefficient hypotheses (the v1 preprint flags development of the general non-torsion formalism; use the rational categorical setting of its stated decomposition), Eis_! = p_!(IC tensor q^* -), CT_* = q_*(IC^-1 tensor p^! -); CT_! = q_!(IC tensor p^* -), Eis_* = p_*(IC tensor q^* -). Adjunctions Eis_! ⊣ CT_* and CT_! ⊣ Eis_* follow by the actual six operations, and CT_{P,!} ≅ CT_{P^-,*} by geometric second adjointness. The unit/counit are compositions of the two standard adjunction units/counits and inverse IC tensor operations; no invented identity of ! and * on arbitrary sheaves. HHS Theorem 1.3.2 and following irreducible-idempotent inclusion give e_irr D contained in the cuspidal part, hence all proper CT_* (and by opposite CT_!) vanish on Aut_alpha with rational coefficients in this formalism. Proper Eisenstein sources are orthogonal to this irreducible object. Thus the C2 proper-parabolic compatibility on the chosen irreducible locus is a vanishing specialization, not a nonzero proper Levi reconstruction or a claim to prove the full equality of cuspidal and irreducible subcategories. R374 arithmetic normalized constant term still useful for reducible parameters.
For a direct sum of original fixed-inertia V_(alpha_i), R317/R339 imply V_alpha ≅ V_beta iff alpha=beta (F^4 scalar, and same construction for equal scalar); Tate twist multiplies loop by 3^4 or its inverse depending on Frobenius convention. Therefore exact LS admission is pairwise alpha_i/alpha_j not in {1,81,1/81}. This permits actual reducible generic blocks of the original family via Zou, with independent scalar stabilizer characters and degree distribution, not one proper splitting of irreducible V_alpha. The R273 generalized Steinberg chain V_alpha tensor C_l(1), l>=2, has Tate-adjacent constituents at the semisimple parameter; this lies OUTSIDE the LS locus and N must be retained. Hence Zou does not pay the nonzero-monodromy C1 or general categorical Eisenstein compatibility on the linked locus. Hansen–Mann arXiv:2606.00983 (2026) gives a broader programme and spectral finiteness/duality; not invoked as an unconditional general theorem here. Separate the local categorical problem from global automorphic globalization.
